2                                          中國金茂控股集團有限公司    二零二五年中期報告
                                       主席致辭
                         CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT




尊敬的各位股東：

本人謹代表中國金茂控股集團有限公司（「中國金茂」、「公司」、「本公司」）的董事會（「董事會」）提呈本公司及其子公司（「本集
團」、「我們」）截至2025年6月30日止六個月（「回顧期」、「報告期」、「2025年上半年」或「上半年」）之中期業績報告，請各位
股東審閱。

上半年，中國經濟運行穩中有進，高質量發展取得新成效，展現出強大活力和韌性。房地產行業競爭格局加速演變，市場分化
加劇，絕大多數城市仍處在深度調整階段，1-6 月全國新建商品房銷售額同比下降5.5%，TOP10房企銷售額同比下滑14%，
行業止跌回穩任重道遠。面臨艱難的外部形勢，公司堅定「做優增量、盤活存量」策略不動搖，着力推進「六力三比」主題年工
作，系統提升全鏈競爭力，銷售業績在行業內率先出現止跌回穩勢頭，銷售規模首進行業前十。

董事會決議向本公司股東派發中期股息每股3港仙，並向股東提供以股代息的選擇。本公司預期於2025年10月31日（星期五）
或之前派發中期股息。本公司將適時公佈中期股息派發（包括以股代息安排）的詳情。

公司持續深化發展戰略，不斷推動組織升級，實現穩健發展。結合市場變化及自身實踐，勾勒出「三步走」戰略藍圖。堅持「深
耕聚焦、提質增效」的戰略主旨，「一核•三聚焦」的業務戰略，逐步明晰了從「活下來」到「活得好」、「活出彩」的戰略藍圖，通
過三年蓄力，實現資產結構合理、各業務平衡發展的煥新目標；通過五年發展，達成效益優良、規模適度、品質領先的戰略目
標，形成抵禦週期波動的金茂發展新模式。找到並驗證了在行業調整期以增量發展帶動存量消化、實現平穩健康發展的路徑。
更大力度推動存量資產盤活，加快資金回流與結構優化；更加極致加速增量項目周轉，迅速回流資金並投入再生產，確保「一
年一熟」，力爭「一年兩熟」。持續推動組織變革，強化穿透式管理，提升經營決策效率。開發業務壓縮管理層級，撤銷五大
區域，調整為「總部 － 地區公司」兩級管控。非開單位持續精簡架構，全面落地「平台+端」模式，減少中間層級，加強平台實
操，打通了「組織變革、效能提升、士氣提振、業績達成」的管理閉環。

公司堅持貫徹經營思維，高效執行，全面兌現業績承諾。兼顧利潤與現金流，存量去化與增量發展並重。以「奮進計劃」為統
領，把握政策窗口期，走通土地盤活的三種創新模式，通過尾盤清盤、老盤新做等方式，加速滯重庫存去化，實現了自我造血
與修復。以「六力三比」為牽引，實現能力迭代升級。堅持優中選優，聚焦核心城市核心板塊獲取16宗優質土地；持續推動「金
玉滿堂」產品線迭代升級，首開16個項目實現量價雙優；落實「6-10-12-24」運營標準，首開、回正、做收週期全面提速。上
半年簽約人民幣533.5億元、排名行業第9位，行業地位穩中有進，現金流總體充裕。加快構建發展新模式，第二曲線穩步發
展。契合城市發展「從大規模增量擴張階段轉向存量提質增效為主的階段」的新趨勢，持有業務業績穩中有升，酒店業績跑贏
競爭組合，長沙金茂覽秀城REIT股價上漲超20%，獲得資本市場認可；物業服務收入同比大幅提升，滿意度達到頭部企業中位
線以上水平，保持高端物業服務地位；建築科技業務圍繞「五衡」發力，持續迭代科技系統產品，鞏固差異化競爭優勢。




                INTERIM REPORT 2025   CHINA JINMAO HOLDINGS GROUP LIMITED   3
                                                          主席致辭
                                                CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT




    Dear shareholders,
    On behalf of the board of directors (the “Board”) of China Jinmao Holdings Group Limited (“China Jinmao” or the
    “Company”), I am pleased to present the interim report of the Company and its subsidiaries (the “Group”, “we” or
    “us”) for the six months ended 30 June 2025 (the “period under review”, “reporting period”, “first half of 2025” or
    “first half of the year”) for your review.
    In the first half of the year, China’s economy maintained steady growth, achieving new results in high-quality
    development and demonstrating strong vitality and resilience. The competitive landscape in the real estate industry
    evolved rapidly, with market differentiation intensifying. Most cities remained in a period of deep adjustment. From
    January to June, national sales amount of newly built commodity housing declined by 5.5% year-on-year, with sales
    amount of the top 10 real estate developers declining by 14% year-on-year. The industry faced a long road ahead in
    stopping the fall and stabilising its performance. Facing a difficult external environment, the Company unswervingly
    adhered to the strategy of “optimising the additional projects and revitalising the existing projects”, and made effort
    to promote the theme year work of “six forces and three benchmarks”, systematically enhancing the competitiveness
    of the entire chain. As a result, the Company was the first in the industry to show the momentum to stop the fall
    and stabilise its sales performance. For the first time, the Company was ranked among the top ten in the industry in
    terms of sales scale.

    The Board resolved to distribute an interim dividend of HK3 cents per share to the shareholders of the Company
    and offer the shareholders an option to receive the interim dividend in the form of scrip shares. The interim dividend
    is expected to be distributed on or before Friday, 31 October 2025. The Company will announce the details of the
    distribution of interim dividend (including scrip dividend arrangements) as and when appropriate.
    The Company continued to deepen its development strategy, and constantly promoted organisational
    upgrades to achieve steady and healthy growth. Combining market changes with its own practices, the
    Company outlined a “three-step” strategic blueprint. Adhering to the strategic purpose of “in-depth cultivation
    and focus, as well as quality and efficiency improvement”, and the business strategy of “One Core • Three Focuses”,
    the Company gradually clarified its strategic blueprint from “survival” to “living well” and “living brilliantly”.
    Through three years of accumulation, the Company would achieve the renewed goal of a reasonable asset structure
    and balanced development of various businesses. Through five years of development, the Company would achieve
    the strategic goal of excellent efficiency, moderate scale and leading quality, forming a new development model
    for Jinmao that is able to withstand cyclical fluctuations. The Company has found and verified a path to make
    use of incremental development to drive the digestion of existing stocks and achieve stable and healthy
    development during the industry adjustment period. By making greater efforts to promote revitalisation of
    existing assets, the Company accelerated funding return and structural optimisation. By accelerating the turnover
    of incremental projects to the extreme, the Company achieved rapid return of funds, which were then invested in
    more production, ensuring “one crop per year” and striving for “two crops per year”. The Company continued to
    promote organisational reform, strengthened penetrating management, and improved the efficiency of
    operational decision-making. For the development business, the Company streamlined its management hierarchy,
    disbanding the five major regions and adjusting them into a two-tiered management system of “headquarters –
    regional companies”. For the non-development business, the Company continued to streamline its structures, and
    fully implemented the “platform + terminal” model, which reduced intermediary ranks and strengthened platform
    operations, creating a closed-loop management system encompassing “organisational reform, improved efficiency,
    boosted morale, and performance”.
    The Company adhered to the implementation of business philosophy and efficient execution, fully
    delivering on its performance commitments. Balancing profits and cash flow, the Company gave equal
    importance to both disposal of existing stocks and incremental development. Guided by “endeavour and
    planning”, it seized the policy window period and implemented the three innovative models of land revitalisation. By
    clearing out the remaining stocks and redeveloping old projects, the Company accelerated the disposal of stagnant
    stocks and achieved self-generation and repair. Leveraging “six forces and three benchmarks”, the Company realised
    iteration and upgrades of capacities. Upholding to the principle of selecting the best from the best, it focused
    on core segments in core cities and acquired 16 high-quality land parcels. Moreover, the Company continuously
    promoted the product lines of “Jin Yu Man Tang” through iteration and upgrades, achieving excellent performance
    in terms of both volume and price for the 16 first-launch projects. The Company also implemented the “6-10-12-
    24” operation standard, fully accelerating the cycle of first opening of sales, cashflow returning to positive and
    completion and delivery of properties. In the first half of the year, the Company achieved a contract sales amount
    of RMB53.35 billion, ranking 9th in the industry, and its industry status made steady progress with sufficient cash
    flow overall. The Company accelerated the construction of a new development model to ensure steady
    development of the second curve. In line with the new trend of city development of “shifting from large-scale
    incremental expansion to focusing on improving the quality and efficiency of existing properties”, the performance
    of holding business has been steadily improving, with the hotel performance outperforming competitors. Changsha
    Jinmao Mall of Splendor REIT’s share price has risen by over 20%, gaining recognition from the capital market.
    Revenue from property services increased significantly year-on-year, and the satisfaction rate reached a level above
    the median of leading enterprises, maintaining the high-end property service status. The building technology
    business focused on the “Five Balances”, and continuously iterated on technological system products to consolidate
    its differentiated competitive advantage.


4                                           中國金茂控股集團有限公司          二零二五年中期報告
                                        主席致辭
                             CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT




公司始終將企業發展融入國家大局，高標準履行社會責任。以品質初心持續引領「好房子」建設。從首個科技住宅「金茂府」的
持續迭代，到「金玉滿堂」產品精彩呈現，今年正式構建了「中國金茂好房子」體系。貫徹「綠色發展」理念，實現高質量發展。
加速超低能耗、綠色建築探索與佈局，助力城市綠色低碳轉型和可持續發展，萬得(Wind)發佈的2025年度ESG評級中，中國金
茂提升至AA級。

中國金茂改革與實踐充分證明了，通過產品引領走出市場獨立行情是行得通的，通過項目成功操盤推動公司組織能力迭代升級
是行得通的，通過做優增量和盤活存量實現三年煥新是行得通的，堅定了我們對未來健康發展的信心和決心。

展望未來，我國房地產市場仍有很大的發展空間，國家提出「更大力度推動房地產市場止跌回穩」，彰顯中央穩定房地產市場的
堅定決心，供需兩端政策利好的預期不斷加強，高質量城市更新與高品質「好房子」建設將給行業企穩向好注入新動能。從行
業競爭來看，未來房地產行業將從完全競爭走向寡頭競爭，對於財務穩健、品質卓越房企是機會大於挑戰。

短期來看，行業仍將處於築底過程中。公司經營要圍繞「三個加快，一個重視」開展：以經營性現金流快速回正為核心，加快
增量項目去化；以實現奮進任務為目標，加快存量項目盤活；以推動意識轉型、業績轉型為重點，加快第二曲線打造；重視風
險隱患控制，助力公司業績兌現與高質量發展。中長期來看，隨着政策持續落地顯效、居民購房信心修復，行業逐步回歸平穩
發展軌道。公司將在加快構建發展「新模式」方面探索佈局。在堅持城市運營商定位的基礎上，把握好存量與增量兩個市場，
持續深化業務戰略內涵。堅持以高品質開發為核心，強化科技賦能，持續夯實「綠色健康、智慧科技」價值標籤，建設安全、
舒適、綠色、智慧的「好房子」，適應人民群眾高品質居住需要，不斷鞏固行業地位。進一步聚焦發展精品持有、品質服務及建
築科技業務，強化服務增值與提效，加速培育業務競爭力。

涅槃煥新，礪行致遠。中國金茂正處在從「活下來」向「活得好」轉變的關鍵期，公司全體員工將永葆創新創業精神，攻堅克
難、砥礪奮進，為全體股東創造更大價值。在此，本人謹代表董事會，向本公司股東、客戶、業務夥伴及社會各界致以誠摯的
謝意！




陶天海
主席
香港
2025年8月26日




               INTERIM REPORT 2025   CHINA JINMAO HOLDINGS GROUP LIMITED   5
                                                          主席致辭
                                                CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT




    The Company has always integrated its development into the broader national context, fulfilling its social
    responsibilities to the highest standards, and continues to lead the development of “good houses” with
    a commitment to quality. From the continuous iteration of the first technological residence “Jinmao Palace”,
    to the wonderful presentation of the “Jin Yu Man Tang” products, the Company formally constructed the system
    of “China Jinmao Good Houses” this year. The Company implemented the concept of “green development”
    and achieved high-quality development. Accelerating the exploration and deployment of ultra-low energy
    consumption and green buildings, the Company assisted in the green and low-carbon transformation and sustainable
    development of cities. China Jinmao was upgraded to AA in the 2025 ESG ratings released by Wind.

    The reforms and practices of China Jinmao have fully demonstrated that it is feasible to lead the market
    to an independent trend by means of products, it is feasible to promote iteration and upgrades of the
    Company’s organisational capabilities through successful project management, and it is feasible to achieve
    three-year renewal by optimising the additional projects and revitalising the existing projects. This has
    strengthened our confidence and determination for healthy development in the future.

    Looking ahead, China’s real estate market still has significant room for growth. The central government has proposed
    “greater efforts to stop the fall and stabilise the real estate market”, demonstrating its resolute determination
    to stabilise the real estate market. Expectations of favourable policies on both the supply and demand sides
    are growing, and high-quality city renewal and the construction of high-quality “good houses” will inject new
    momentum into the industry’s stabilisation and growth. From the perspective of industry competition, the future of
    the real estate industry will move from full competition towards oligopoly competition, presenting more opportunities
    than challenges for financially sound and high-quality real estate enterprises.

    In the short term, the industry will still be in the process of bottoming out. The Company’s operations
    should focus on “three accelerations and one emphasis”: with the rapid recovery of operating cashflow as the
    core, to accelerate the disposal of incremental projects; with the goal of achieving progressive mission, to accelerate
    the revitalisation of existing projects; with the focus on promoting awareness transformation and performance
    transformation, to accelerate the creation of second curve; emphasis should be put on control of risks and hidden
    dangers to help the Company achieve performance and high-quality development. In the medium to long term,
    as policies continue to take effect and residents’ confidence in purchasing houses recovers, the industry
    will gradually return to a stable development track. The Company will explore and deploy strategies to
    accelerate the construction of a “new model” for development. On the basis of adhering to the positioning
    of a city operator, the Company will grasp both existing and incremental markets and continue to deepen the
    connotation of business strategies. Upholding high-quality development as the core, the Company will strengthen
    technological empowerment, continue to consolidate the value label of “green health, smart technology”, build
    safe, comfortable, green and smart “good houses”, meet the people’s needs for high-quality living, and continuously
    consolidate its industry position. The Company will also further focus on developing premium holding, quality service
    and building technology businesses, strengthen service value-added and efficiency improvement, and accelerate the
    cultivation of business competitiveness.

    Reborn with new vitality, striving forward with perseverance to reach far. China Jinmao is in a critical period
    of transformation from “survival” to “living well”. All employees of the Company will always maintain the spirit of
    innovation and entrepreneurship, overcome difficulties and forge ahead, creating greater value for all shareholders.
    On behalf of the Board, I would like to express my sincere gratitude to the shareholders, customers, business
    partners of the Company and various sectors of the community.




    TAO Tianhai
    Chairman
    Hong Kong
    26 August 2025

     4 號線六緯路站上蓋。作為海河東岸的標誌     District, and above Liuwei Road Station of Metro Line No.4. As a
     性建築，金茂匯是在原天津第一熱電廠的基      landmark building (the original Tianjin No.1 Thermal Power Plant) on
     礎上對標倫敦巴特西電站進行轉化更新的，      the east bank of Hai River, Jinmao Place was transformed and updated
     在整體設計策略上，項目以「修舊如舊、新      to benchmark Battersea Power Station in London. In terms of the
     舊並置」的理念，盡可能保留老建築的原始      overall design strategy, the project follows the concept of “repairing
     結構美學，通過對廠房紅色磚牆的復原、頂      the old as it was, juxtaposing the old with the new” by retaining the
     部桁架的加固以及原有煤鬥群落的修復，重      original structural aesthetics of the old building as much as possible,
     新為工業遺蹟注入生命力。在充分發掘在地      and re-injecting vitality into the industrial relics through restoration
     文化的基礎上，金茂匯還積極打造海河東岸      of the red brick walls of the factory building, reinforcement of the
     的商業場景，引入多家城市首店及區域首       top trusses, and restoration of the original coal hopper community.
     店；也吸引了諸多知名連鎖品牌聯合開發打      On the basis of fully exploring local culture, Jinmao Place has also
     造專屬定制主題店。                actively created a commercial scene on the east bank of Hai River by
                              introducing many first stores in the city and region and attracting
                              many well-known chain brands to jointly develop exclusive and
                              customised theme stores.

     回顧期內，天津金茂匯為更多商業品牌首次      During the period under review, Tianjin Jinmao Place opened up space
     進駐天津開拓了空間，未來還將重新啟動所      for more commercial brands to enter Tianjin for the first time. In the
     在區域的商業氛圍，助力海河沿岸東岸消費      future, it will also reactivate business vibe in the area, helping the
     走廊建設，帶動天津中心城區進一步更新提      construction of a consumption corridor along the east bank of Hai

服務                               Services

               「共生」三             Jinmao Services actively built the three core IPs of “quality”,
大核心IP，堅持以客戶為中心，提供卓越的             “intelligence” and “symbiosis”, and adhered to the customer-
場景設計能力和穩定的品質輸出能力。通過              oriented principle, providing excellent scenario design capabilities and
萬物互聯管設備、移動互聯管理服務，金茂              stable quality output capabilities. Through the Internet of Everything
服務深入推動企業數字化轉型，以科技促進              management equipment and mobile Internet management services,
服務升級，持續提升管理服務效率和用戶數              Jinmao Services deeply promoted the digital transformation of
字化體驗。同時，金茂服務積極做強社群生              enterprises, drove service upgrading with technology, and continuously
態，拓展城市服務，引領低碳、環保、綠色              improved the management service efficiency and user digital
的可持續發展理念，以服務承擔社會責任，              experience. At the same time, Jinmao Services actively strengthened
提升人民幸福感，與社區、城市、環境、社              the community ecosystem, expanded city services, led the sustainable
會共生共榮。                           development concept of low-carbon, environmental protection and
                                 green, assumed social responsibilities with services, enhanced people’s
                                 happiness, and grew and prospered together with communities, cities,
                                 environment, and the society.

科技                               Technology
中國金茂聚焦建築科技，着力培育專精特新              China Jinmao focuses on construction technology, striving to cultivate
業務，強化主業技術護城河，夯實差異化競              specialised and new businesses, strengthening the technical moat
爭優勢。回顧期內，圍繞綠色、低碳、智               for its main business, and consolidating its differentiated competitive
能、安全的「好房子」，中國金茂繼續夯實科             advantages. During the period under review, with a focus on the
技人居系統和減振降噪等全流程一體化EPC             “good houses” of green, low-carbon, smart, and secured elements,
服務模式，推動金茂府系3.0 「科技」硬核不           China Jinmao continued to consolidate the full-process integrated
斷升級；智慧能源板塊聚焦綜合能源服務和              EPC service model such as the technological living system and
綠色大數據中心業務，着力提升經營效益，              vibration and noise reduction, promoting the continuous upgrade
持續提升社會環保效益，為達成「碳達峰」、             of the hardcore technology of Jinmao Palace series 3.0. As for the
「碳中和」的目標貢獻企業力量。                  smart energy segment, it focused on comprehensive energy services
                                 and green big data centre business, striving to improve operating
                                 efficiency and continuing to improve social environmental benefits,
                                 thus contributing to the goals of “carbon peaking” and “carbon
                                 neutrality”.

未來展望                             FUTURE OUTLOOK

展望未來，我國房地產市場仍有很大的發展              Looking ahead, China’s real estate market still has significant room
空間，國家提出「更大力度推動房地產市場              for growth. The central government has proposed “greater efforts
止跌回穩」，彰顯中央穩定房地產市場的堅              to stop the fall and stabilise the real estate market”, demonstrating
定決心，供需兩端政策利好的預期不斷加               its resolute determination to stabilise the real estate market.
強，高質量城市更新與高品質「好房子」建設             Expectations of favourable policies on both the supply and demand
將給行業企穩向好注入新動能。從行業競爭              sides are growing, and high-quality city renewal and the construction
來看，未來房地產行業將從完全競爭走向寡              of high-quality “good houses” will inject new momentum into the
頭競爭，對於財務穩健、品質卓越房企是機              industry’s stabilisation and growth. From the perspective of industry
會大於挑戰。                           competition, the future of the real estate industry will move from
                                 full competition towards oligopoly competition, presenting more
                                 opportunities than challenges for financially sound and high-quality
                                 real estate enterprises.

     二十七、市場風險                      XXVII. Market risk
     本集團的資產主要為發展中土地、發展中物           The Group’s assets are predominantly in the form of land under
     業、持作出售物業及投資物業。倘國內物業           development, properties under development, properties held for sale
     市場嚴重回落，則該等資產未必可實時變            and investment properties. In the event of a severe downturn in the
     現。                            property market in Mainland China, these assets may not be readily
                                   realised.

     二十八、利率風險                      XXVIII. Interest rate risk
     本集團面臨因利率波動而產生的利率風險。           The Group is exposed to interest rate risk resulting from fluctuations
     本集團所面臨的市場利率變動風險主要與本           in interest rates. The Group’s exposure to the risk of changes in
     集團的長期債務有關。利率上升會導致本集           market interest rates relates primarily to the Group’s long-term
     團尚未償還浮動利率借款的利息支出增加，           debt obligations. Increase in interest rates will increase the interest
     並增加新增債務的成本。利率的波動還會導           expenses relating to the Group’s outstanding floating rate borrowings
     致本集團債務欠款公平值的大幅波動。董事           and increase the cost of new debt. Fluctuations in interest rates may
     會將不時審視本集團的利率風險，並將於有           also lead to significant fluctuations in the fair value of the Group’s
     需要時利用對沖，以控制利率風險。              debt obligations. The Board will from time to time review the interest
                                   rate risk of the Group and will hedge and control such risks should
                                   the need arise.

二十九、外匯風險                       XXIX. Foreign currency exchange risk
本集團大部分營業收入和成本均以人民幣結            Substantially all of the Group’s revenue and costs are denominated
算，本集團中期報告為以人民幣呈報財務業            in RMB. The interim report of the Group uses RMB as presentation
績。由於本集團仍持有美元和港幣借款，因            currency of its financial results. The Group still has borrowings
此，本集團面臨匯率波動的風險。董事會將            denominated in USD and HKD. As a result, the Group is exposed to
不時審視本集團的外匯風險，並將於有需要            the risk of fluctuations in foreign exchange rates. The Board will from
時利用對沖，以控制外匯風險。                 time to time review the foreign exchange exposure risk of the Group
                               and will hedge and control such risks should the need arise.

三十、網絡風險及安全                     XXX. Network risks and security
隨著計算機系統及互聯網網絡在我們的運營            As the computer system and the Internet play a key role in our
中發揮重要作用，本集團已指定專業人士監            operations, the Group has designated professionals to monitor
控及評估潛在的網絡風險。硬件及軟件均按            and assess the potential network risks. Hardware and software are
照適當的公司政策追蹤。潛在的網絡風險及            subject to tracking according to appropriate policies of the Company.
網絡安全是管理層關注的主要問題之一，因            Potential network risks and network security are major issues that
此本集團已指定政策及程序來規範互聯網的            draw the management’s attention. Accordingly, the Group has put
使用、系統供電的實質維護、定期更新互聯            in place policies and procedures to regulate the use of the Internet,
網保安系統及防火牆，以將公司內部網絡與            physical maintenance of system power supply and regular update
外界網絡隔離。指定的專業人士負責對任何            of the Internet security system and firewall to isolate the Company’s
異常的網絡活動進行日常監控。                 intranet from outside networks. Designated professionals are
                               responsible for daily monitoring of any unusual network activities.

的內部監控系統。本集團已採用適當政策保            on data and information access. The Group has adopted appropriate
護數據，只允許獲授權人士登入。管理層認            policies to protect its data. Only authorised persons are allowed

據欺詐風險。                         procedures are effectively implemented to avoid data fraud and theft
                               risks.

     司之承擔，以及努力實踐本公司之目標。             commitment of the participants to the Company and encourage them

     為4年。                           directors of the Company. As at the end of the reporting period, the

審閱範圍                             SCOPE OF REVIEW

我們已按照香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審閱              We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard
工作準則第2410 號「實體之獨立核數師對中           on Review Engagements 2410 Review of Interim Financial Information
期財務數據之審閱 」的規定進行審閱，審閱             Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity as issued by the
中期財務資料包括主要向負責財務會計事宜              HKICPA. A review of interim financial information consists of making
的人員進行詢問，並實施分析性覆核和其他              inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and accounting
審閱程序。該審閱工作範圍遠小於根據香港              matters, and applying analytical and other review procedures. A
審計準則進行審計工作的範圍，我們因而無              review is substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in
法保證能在審閱工作中發現若進行審計工作              accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing and consequently
的情況下所能發現的所有重大事項。因此，              does not enable us to obtain assurance that we would become
我們不發表審計意見。                       aware of all significant matters that might be identified in an audit.
                                 Accordingly, we do not express an audit opinion.

結論                               CONCLUSION

根據我們的審閱，我們並無發現任何事項使              Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that causes
我們相信中期財務資料未能在所有重大方面              us to believe that the interim financial information is not prepared, in
按照香港會計準則第34號的規定編製。               all material respects, in accordance with HKAS 34.

     3. 會計政策及披露的變動                           3. CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND
                                                DISCLOSURES

     編製中期簡明綜合財務資料所採納的會計政                     The accounting policies adopted in the preparation of the interim
     策與編製本集團截至2024 年12 月31 日止年               condensed consolidated financial information are consistent with
     度的年度綜合財務報表所應用的一致，惟本                     those applied in the preparation of the Group’s annual consolidated
     集團於本期間的財務資料中首次採納以下經                     financial statements for the year ended 31 December 2024, except for
     修訂香港財務報告準則會計準則除外。                       the adoption of the following amended HKFRS Accounting Standard
                                             for the first time for the current period’s financial information.


     香港會計準則               缺乏可兌換性             Amendments to HKAS 21             Lack of Exchangeability
      第21號修訂本

     經修訂香港財務報告準則會計準則的性質及                     The nature and impact of the amended HKFRS Accounting Standard
     影響載列如下：                                 are described below:

     香港會計準則第21號修訂本訂明實體應如何                    Amendments to HKAS 21 specify how an entity shall assess whether
     評估某種貨幣是否可兌換為另一種貨幣，以                     a currency is exchangeable into another currency and how it
     及於缺乏可兌換性的情況下，實體應如何估                     shall estimate a spot exchange rate at a measurement date when
     計計量日期的即期匯率。該等修訂要求披露                     exchangeability is lacking. The amendments require disclosures of
     使財務報表使用者能夠了解貨幣不可兌換的                     information that enable users of financial statements to understand
     影響的資料。由於本集團進行交易的貨幣與                     the impact of a currency not being exchangeable. As the currencies
     集團實體用於換算為本集團呈列貨幣的功能                     that the Group had transacted with and the functional currencies of
     貨幣可互相兌換，該等修訂本並無對中期簡                     group entities for translation into the Group’s presentation currency
     明綜合財務資料產生任何影響。                          were exchangeable, the amendments did not have any impact on the
                                             interim condensed consolidated financial information.

管理層會就資源分配及表現評估的決策，分               Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating segments
開監控本集團經營分部的業績。分部表現乃               separately for the purpose of making decisions about resources
按可呈報分部利潤進行評估，此乃按照經調               allocation and performance assessment. Segment performance is
整稅前利潤計量。經調整稅前利潤的計量與               evaluated based on reportable segment profit, which is a measure of
本集團稅前利潤相符，惟於有關計算中並無               adjusted profit before tax. The adjusted profit before tax is measured
計入利息收入、其他投資收入及非租賃相關               consistently with the Group’s profit before tax except that interest
融資成本以及其他未分配總部及企業開支。               income, other investment income and non-lease related finance costs
                                  as well as other unallocated head office and corporate expenses are
                                  excluded from such measurement.

受限制銀行結餘、現金和現金等價物、若干               bank balances, cash and cash equivalents, certain other financial
其他金融資產及其他未分配總部及企業資                assets and other unallocated head office and corporate assets.
產。

分部負債不包括計息銀行及其他借款以及相               Segment liabilities exclude interest-bearing bank and other borrowings
關應付利息、應付稅項、土地增值稅撥備、               and related interest payables, tax payable, provision for land

1 發行境內公司債券                          1       Issue of domestic corporate bonds
於 2025 年 2 月 21 日，本公司全資附屬公司         On 21 February 2025, Shanghai Jinmao, a wholly-owned subsidiary of
上海金茂作為發行人完成向專業投資者發行                 the Company (as the issuer) completed the issuance of the corporate

獨立董事委員會關於世貿投資選擇                            REVIEW OF THE OPTION OVER SHIMAO INVESTMENT
權之檢討（續）                                    BY THE INDEPENDENT BOARD COMMITTEE (CONTINUED)

 獨立董事委員會仔細審閱世貿投資的相關資                       The Independent Board Committee has carefully reviewed the relevant
 料，並考慮中化集團現時為世貿投資的財務                       information of Shimao Investment, taking into account the facts that
 投資者，僅佔少數及被動性權益，且世貿投                       Sinochem Group is a financial investor of Shimao Investment and
 資旗下物業業態以寫字樓為主，均與公司佈                       owns only a minority and passive interest in it, and that the property
 局「消費類基礎設施」商業業態及著力打造                       business of Shimao Investment is mainly office buildings, which is
「 覽 秀 城 」品 牌 的 戰 略 不 符 。 本 公 司 目 前 整       inconsistent with the Company’s strategy of developing the “consumer
 體總債務規模維持穩健，在當前地產行業波                       infrastructure” commercial business and focusing on building the
 動較大的情況下，公司應堅持通過「收斂聚                       “Mall of Splendor” brand. At present, the Company’s overall total
 焦、提質增效」夯實主營業務，如此時收購                       debt position remains stable. In face of the higher volatility in the real
 世貿投資，將對公司的人力財力等各方面提                       estate industry at present, the Company should insist on consolidating
 出更大挑戰。因此，獨立非執行董事一致認                       its principal businesses by “focus augmentation, as well as quality
 為，現時行使對世貿投資的選擇權並不符合                       and efficiency improvement”. The acquisition of Shimao Investment
 本公司穩健投資的政策、不符合本公司及股                       at the moment would pose greater challenges to the Company in
 東的整體最佳利益。                                 various aspects, including manpower and financial capacity. As such,
                                           the independent non-executive directors unanimously agreed that the
                                           exercise of the option over Shimao Investment at this moment is not
                                           in line with the Company’s prudent investment policy, and is not in
                                           the best interests of the Company and the shareholders as a whole.

獨立董事委員會一致決定，目前仍暫不行使                        The Independent Board Committee unanimously resolved not to
選擇權藉以收購中化集團於世貿投資15%的                       exercise the option to acquire Sinochem Group’s 15% interest in
權益。誠如招股書及本公司2010 年 4 月 9 日                 Shimao Investment at this moment. As disclosed in the prospectus
發佈的公告所述，獨立董事委員會將繼續不                        and the announcement dated 9 April 2010 of the Company, the
時檢討對世貿投資的選擇權，並於本公司中                        Independent Board Committee would continue to review the option
期及年度報告中做出相關披露。                             over Shimao Investment and make relevant disclosure in the interim
                                           and annual reports of the Company.
